# Warships -- the map

Generated by `python tools/map.py`; do not edit. "Used by" is a whole-word match on the name, so a
name two classes share is credited to both -- the build, the analysers and `tools/analyse/xref.py`
are what prove something unused.

82 scripts, 18878 lines; 170 types; 3901 members; 33 RPCs; 144 node spawn sites; 149 asset files.
